Assignment is unrestricted. The SOW has expired.\"\n    obs.append({\"source_path\": path, \"location\": \"body\", \"excerpt\": ex})\n  elif marker.startswith(\"## 8.\"):\n    ex = excerpt_section(text, \"## 8. Exclusivity\")\n    if not ex and \"Exclusivity\" not in text:\n      ex = excerpt_header(text, (\"Counterparty:\", \"Term:\")) + \"\\n\\n[No exclusivity section in contract]\"\n    obs.append({\"source_path\": path, \"location\": marker, \"excerpt\": ex or \"[section not found]\"})\n  elif marker.startswith(\"##\"):\n    ex = excerpt_section(text, marker if \"Exclusivity\" in marker else marker)\n    if not ex:\n      first = next((l for l in text.splitlines() if marker.split()[0] in l), None)\n      ex = first or \"[section not found]\"\n    obs.append({\"source_path\": path, \"location\": marker, \"excerpt\": ex})\n  else:\n    ex = excerpt_line(text, marker)\n    if ex:\n      obs.append({\"source_path\": path, \"location\": marker, \"excerpt\": ex})\n  return obs\n\nobligations = json.loads(OBL_PATH.read_text())\nfor obl in obligations:\n  oid = obl[\"obligation_id\"]\n  rel = obl[\"relation\"]\n  vals = obl[\"values\"]\n  packet = {\"obligation_id\": oid, \"selected_observations\": [], \"selection_rationale\": \"\", \"known_missing_information\": \"\"}\n\n  if rel == \"acquisition_clause_judgment\":\n    contract, kind = vals[\"contract\"], vals[\"kind\"]\n    key = (contract, kind)\n    packet[\"selected_observations\"] = make_acquisition_obs(contract, ACQUISITION_SECTIONS[key])\n    packet[\"selection_rationale\"] = ACQUISITION_RATIONALE[kind]\n    packet[\"known_missing_information\"] = ACQUISITION_MISSING.get(key, \"Full contract body beyond excerpted sections not included.\")\n\n  elif rel == \"active_contract_judgment\":\n    contract = vals[\"contract\"]\n    packet[\"selected_observations\"] = ACTIVE_OBS[contract]\n    packet[\"selection_rationale\"] = ACTIVE_RATIONALE[contract]\n
